expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Question 4" })).toBeInTheDocument();\n  });\n\n  it("jumps to any question that is not the one already open", () => {\n    const onJumpTo = vi.fn();\n    render(<AnswerOvals {...base} onJumpTo={onJumpTo} />);\n\n    // Forwards to an unanswered question, and backwards to an answered one:\n    // a fixed queue walks in both directions, not just expect(onJumpTo).toHaveBeenNthCalledWith(1, 3);\n    expect(onJumpTo).toHaveBeenNthCalledWith(2, 0);\n  });\n\n  it("does not offer the current question as somewhere to jump", () => {\n    const onJumpTo = vi.fn();\n    render(<AnswerOvals {...base} onJumpTo={onJumpTo} />);\n\n    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Question 2, answered, current" }));\n\n    expect(onJumpTo).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n  });\n\n  it("explains itself when jumping is off rather than going quietly dead", () => {\n    const onJumpTo = vi.fn();\n    render(\n      <AnswerOvals\n        {...base}\n        jumpEnabled={false}\n        jumpDisabledReason="Paused — resume to navigate"\n        onJumpTo={onJumpTo}\n      />,\n    );\n\n    const oval = screen.getByRole("button", Verified: token issued, and `booking_passes` read back through RLS as admin.\r\nThis covers essentially all **data**-level work — reading and writing rows, calling RPCs like\r\n`grant_booking_access` and `regenerate_booking_link`, driving the booking/homework/messaging\r\nadmin functions. The JWT expires in 3600s; re-auth rather than caching it.\r\n\r\n**CANNOT do — genuinely Lovable-UI-only, Joshua must drive:** DDL and migrations, secrets,\r\nstorage file browsing/download, backups and restore, instance sizing, auth provider config,\r\nDB exports. Note pushed migrations do **not** auto-apply — two booking migrations sat unapplied\r\non 2026-07-19 while edge functions synced.\r\n\r\n**HAZARD — never resolve the project by name.** The connected Supabase MCP sees only\r\nJoshua\'s *own* org `ostsvacinxuehptsniry`, which does not contain the live backend but does\r\ncontain a different project *also named "LSATU"* (`tlqltiklhmrceksqnwsl`, ACTIVE_HEALTHY).\r\nSQL aimed at "the LSATU project" via the MCP applies cleanly to the wrong database and\r\nreports success. Match the ref `atrcqxpmqrynmtvvyjju` exactly, or do not run it. The MCP is\r\nnot a path to this backend at all.\r\n\r\n**Notes / gaps:**\r\n- Task tracking is deliberately the `wiki/tasks.md` Now/Next/Done ledger, not an external tool (ClickUp/Asana). This is the intended design — the wiki is the tracker.\r\n- Session-note ingestion (Domain 6) is the highest-leverage under-used pipe: ~191 notes/yr, <10% currently ingested. Math.min(Math.max(requestedInitialIndex, 0), Math.max(questionQueue.length - 1, 0))\r\n        : 0;\r\n      let restoredAttempts: DrillSession['attempts'] = new Map();\r\n      // Set only when a stored section had already run out of time.\r\n      let restoredRegulationMark: DrillSession['regulationMark'] | undefined;\r\n\r\n      // Intro to LR practice sets resume from durable storage, not from the URL.\r\n      // A student who closed the tab, signed out, or moved to another device\r\n      // comes back to the question they stopped on with every prior answer\r\n      // still marked answered in the navigator.\r\n      if (isMcPractice && mcPracticePool && user) {\r\n        const stored = await loadPracticeSession(user.id, mcPracticePool.slug);\r\n        const sameSet =\r\n          !!stored &&\r\n          stored.status === 'in_progress' &&\r\n          stored.question_queue.length === questionQueue.length &&\r\n          stored.question_queue.every((qid) => questionQueue.includes(qid));\r\n\r\n        if (stored && sameSet) {\r\n          questionQueue = stored.question_queue;\r\n          initialIndex = Math.min(\r\n            Math.max(stored.current_index, 0),\r\n            Math.max(questionQueue.length - 1, 0),\r\n          );\r\n          for (const attempt of stored.attempts) {\r\n            restoredAttempts.set(attempt.qid, {\r\n              selectedAnswer: attempt.selected_answer,\r\n              correct: attempt.correct,\r\n              timeMs: attempt.time_ms,\r\n              timestamp: Date.now(),\r\n              confidence: undefined,\r\n              reviewDone: false,\r\n              // The record, not just the answer. A resumed run used to come\r\n              // back flattened: no changes of mind, no marks for review.\r\n              switchCount: attempt.switch_count,\r\n              brMarked: attempt.flagged,\r\n              answerRevealed: attempt.answer_revealed ?? false,\r\n              answerTrail: attempt.answer_trail?.map((e) => ({\r\n                answer: e.answer,\r\n                atMs: e.at_ms,\r\n              })),\r\n            });\r\n          }\r\n        } else if (stored && !sameSet && stored.status === 'in_progress') {\r\n          // The frozen pool changed under a saved run. Starting clean is the\r\n          // only honest option; say so rather than silently dropping answers.\r\n          console.warn('[drill:mc-practice] stored resume set no longer matches pool, starting fresh', {\r\n            slug: mcPracticePool.slug,\r\n          });\r\n        }\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      // Guided drills resume the same way, and for the same reason: leaving a\r\n      // drill and coming back must return the student to the question they\r\n      // stopped on with their earlier answers intact, not to a fresh shuffle.\r\n      if (guidedSessionKey && user) {\r\n        const stored = await loadGuidedDrillSession(\r\n          user.id,\r\n          guidedSessionKey,\r\n          guidedLegacySessionKey,\r\n        );\r\n        if (isResumableGuidedSession(stored, questionQueue)) {\r\n          questionQueue = stored.question_queue;\r\n          initialIndex = guidedResumeIndex(\r\n            questionQueue,\r\n            stored.current_index,\r\n            stored.attempts.map((a) => a.qid),\r\n          );\r\n          for (const attempt of stored.attempts) {\r\n            restoredAttempts.set(attempt.qid, {\r\n              selectedAnswer: attempt.selected_answer,\r\n              correct: attempt.correct,\r\n              timeMs: attempt.time_ms,\r\n              timestamp: Date.now(),\r\n              confidence: undefined,\r\n              reviewDone: false,\r\n              // The record, not just the answer. A resumed run used to come\r\n              // back flattened: no changes of mind, no marks for review.\r\n              switchCount: attempt.switch_count,\r\n              brMarked: attempt.flagged,\r\n              answerRevealed: attempt.answer_revealed ?? false,\r\n              answerTrail: attempt.answer_trail?.map((e) => ({\r\n                answer: e.answer,\r\n                atMs: e.at_ms,\r\n              })),\r\n            });\r\n          }\r\n        } else if (stored?.status === 'in_progress') {\r\n          console.warn('[drill:guided] stored resume set no longer matches this drill, starting fresh', {\r\n            key: guidedSessionKey,\r\n          });\r\n        }\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      // Smart Drill, custom type drills and full sections. These are drawn\r\n      // fresh on every launch, so the stored queue IS the run: adopt it whole\r\n      // rather than comparing it to a new shuffle, or the student loses the\r\n      // questions they already answered. An explicit ?initialIndex wins, since\r\n      // that is someone deliberately jumping to a question.\r\n      if (genericResumeKey && user && !initialIndexParam) {\r\n        const stored = await loadDrillResumeSession(\r\n          user.id,\r\n          genericResumeKey,\r\n          genericLegacyResumeKey,\r\n        );\r\n        // A finished assignment's row is that sitting's record. This mount\r\n        // must never write over it: a reload during blind review re-mounts\r\n        // the runner with a fresh clock and no answers, and the first answer\r\n        // given on it used to flip the finished row back to in_progress.\r\n        if (assignmentId && stored?.status === 'completed') {\r\n          genericFinalizedRef.current = true;\r\n        }\r\n        if (isResumableDrillSession(stored)) {\r\n          // Come back on the clock they left. A timed section that resumed\r\n          // with a fresh 35:00 was handing back time already spent, which\r\n          // makes the score it produces meaningless.\r\n          if (stored?.elapsed_ms) pendingResumeElapsedMs.current = stored.elapsed_ms;\r\n          if (stored?.regulation_answered_qids) {\r\n            restoredRegulationMark = { answeredQids: stored.regulation_answered_qids };\r\n          }\r\n          // A row stored at or after the buzzer has no clock left to resume\r\n          // on. It opens on the buzzer instead: answers kept, no time handed\r\n          // back, and the student is asked to finish and score the section.\r\n          const budget =\r\n            mode === 'full-section'\r\n              ? sectionBudgetMs((state.config as FullSectionConfig | undefined)?.timer)\r\n              : undefined;\r\n          if (isSpentDrillSession(stored, budget)) {\r\n            pendingResumeAtBuzzer.current = true;\r\n            if (!restoredRegulationMark) {\r\n              restoredRegulationMark = { answeredQids: stored!.attempts.map((a) => a.qid) };\r\n            }\r\n          }\r\n          questionQueue = stored.question_queue;\r\n          initialIndex = guidedResumeIndex(\r\n            questionQueue,\r\n            stored.current_index,\r\n            stored.attempts.map((a) => a.qid),\r\n          );\r\n          for (const attempt of stored.attempts) {\r\n            restoredAttempts.set(attempt.qid, {\r\n              selectedAnswer: attempt.selected_answer,\r\n              correct: attempt.correct,\r\n              timeMs: attempt.time_ms,\r\n              timestamp: Date.now(),\r\n              confidence: undefined,\r\n              reviewDone: false,\r\n              // The record, not just the answer. [{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 7.2 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '/**\r\n * Resume state for every OTHER drill — Smart Drill, custom type drills, and\r\n * full sections.\r\n *\r\n * Guided drills and Intro to LR practice sets already survive a closed tab, a\r\n * sign-out, or a different device (see guidedDrillSession). Nothing else did:\r\n * a student mid-way through a Smart Drill who closed the browser came back to\r\n * a fresh shuffle, and the answers they had already given were nowhere on the\r\n * screen. The attempts themselves were never lost — those are written to the\r\n * `attempts` table per question — but the run was, which reads as lost work.\r\n *\r\n * This rides the exact same store as the other two, namespaced so the three can\r\n * never collide:\r\n *\r\n *     practice set   →  pool_slug "mc-practice-1"\r\n *     guided drill   →  pool_slug "guided::<assignmentId>"\r\n *     bootcamp day   →  pool_slug "drill::bootcamp-day::<day>::<part>"\r\n *     homework       →  pool_slug "drill::assignment::<assignmentId>"\r\n *     everything else→  pool_slug "drill::<mode>::<fingerprint>"\r\n *\r\n * Failure policy, inherited: reads degrade to null (a missing resume row must\r\n * never block starting a drill), writes surface to the caller.\r\n */\r\n\r\nimport {\r\n  loadPracticeSession,\r\n  savePracticeSessionSnapshot,\r\n  completePracticeSession,\r\n  type PracticeSessionSnapshot,\r\n} from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/lib/practiceSessionStore\';\r\nimport type { PracticeSession } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/persistence/records\';\r\n\r\nexport const DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX = \'drill::\';\r\n\r\nexport type DrillResumeSession = PracticeSession;\r\nexport type DrillResumeSnapshot = PracticeSessionSnapshot;\r\n\r\nexport type DrillResumeIdentity = {\r\n  kind: \'bootcamp-day\';\r\n  day: number;\r\n  part: \'homework\' | \'practice\';\r\n};\r\n\r\n/** Short, stable, filename-safe digest of whatever identifies the run. */\r\nfunction fingerprint(input: string): string {\r\n  let h = 2166136261;\r\n  for (let i = 0; i < input.length; i++) {\r\n    h ^= input.charCodeAt(i);\r\n    h = Math.imul(h, 16777619);\r\n  }\r\n  return (h >>> 0).toString(36);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Key one run\'s resume row.\r\n *\r\n * A homework assignment is identified by the assignment: two assignments of\r\n * the same section are two sittings, and a row named by the section alone let\r\n * the second one open on the first one\'s answers and the first one\'s spent\r\n * clock. Otherwise a full section is identified by the section itself; a\r\n * filtered drill by the filters that drew it; Smart Drill by nothing but its\r\n * mode, because it has a single continuous run per student. Returns null for\r\n * the surfaces that own their own resume rows (guided, practice sets) and for\r\n * anything launched from an explicit list of qids, where the caller already\r\n * knows the set.\r\n */\r\nexport function drillResumeKey(input: {\r\n  mode: string;\r\n  config?: unknown;\r\n  identity?: DrillResumeIdentity;\r\n  /** The classroom homework assignment this run belongs to, when it does. */\r\n  assignmentId?: string | null;\r\n}): string | null {\r\n  if (input.assignmentId) {\r\n    return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}assignment::${input.assignmentId}`;\r\n  }\r\n  if (input.identity?.kind === \'bootcamp-day\') {\r\n    return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}bootcamp-day::${input.identity.day}::${input.identity.part}`;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  const mode = input.mode;\r\n  if (mode === \'adaptive\') return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}adaptive`;\r\n\r\n  const config = (input.config ?? null) as Record<string, unknown> | null;\r\n\r\n  if (mode === \'full-section\') {\r\n    if (!config) return null;\r\n    const pt = config.pt;\r\n    const section = config.section;\r\n    if (pt == null || section == null) return null;\r\n    return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}section::pt${pt}-s${section}`;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  if (mode === \'type-drill\' || mode === \'natural-drill\') {\r\n    if (!config) return null;\r\n    const selected = config.selectedQids as string[] | undefined;\r\n    if (selected?.length) {\r\n      // A pre-selected set is its own identity: the same list re-opened is the\r\n      // same run, a different list is a different one.\r\n      return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}${mode}::set-${fingerprint([...selected].sort().join(\',\'))}`;\r\n    }\r\n    const shape = JSON.stringify({\r\n      qtypes: config.qtypes ?? [],\r\n      difficulties: config.difficulties ?? [],\r\n      pts: config.pts ?? [],\r\n      sections: config.sections ?? [],\r\n      qfeatures: config.qfeatures ?? [],\r\n      reasoningTypes: config.reasoningTypes ?? [],\r\n      searchQuery: config.searchQuery ?? \'\',\r\n      questionNumber: config.questionNumber ?? null,\r\n      count: config.count ?? null,\r\n    });\r\n    return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}${mode}::${fingerprint(shape)}`;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  return null;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** The stored run for this drill, or null when there is nothing to resume. */\r\nexport async function loadDrillResumeSession(\r\n  userId: string,\r\n  key: string | null,\r\n  legacyKey?: string | null,\r\n): Promise<DrillResumeSession | null> {\r\n  if (!userId || !key) return null;\r\n  const current = await loadPracticeSession(userId, key);\r\n  if (current || !legacyKey || legacyKey === key) return current;\r\n  return loadPracticeSession(userId, legacyKey);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** Write where the student is right now (local-first, debounced cloud upsert). */\r\nexport function saveDrillResumeSnapshot(\r\n  userId: string,\r\n  key: string | null,\r\n  snapshot: DrillResumeSnapshot,\r\n): Promise<void> {\r\n  if (!userId || !key) return Promise.resolve();\r\n  return savePracticeSessionSnapshot(userId, { slug: key, completionId: key }, snapshot);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Mark the run finished. Nothing is deleted — the queue and every answer stay\r\n * in the row for review — it simply stops being offered as a resume, so the\r\n * next launch of the same drill draws fresh questions.\r\n */\r\nexport function completeDrillResumeSession(\r\n  userId: string,\r\n  key: string | null,\r\n  snapshot: DrillResumeSnapshot,\r\n): Promise<void> {\r\n  if (!userId || !key) return Promise.resolve();\r\n  return completePracticeSession(userId, { slug: key, completionId: key }, snapshot);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Is this stored row worth resuming?\r\n *\r\n * Unlike a guided drill, these runs are drawn fresh on every launch, so there\r\n * is no fixed set to compare against — the stored queue IS the run. It is\r\n * resumable when it is still in progress and the student actually answered\r\n * something; a row with no answers has nothing to restore and would only\r\n * freeze a stale shuffle in place.\r\n */\r\nexport function isResumableDrillSession(stored: DrillResumeSession | null): boolean {\r\n  return (\r\n    !!stored &&\r\n    stored.status === \'in_progress\' &&\r\n    stored.question_queue.length > 0 &&\r\n    stored.attempts.length > 0\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Was this row\'s section clock already spent when it was stored?\r\n *\r\n * A row written at or after the buzzer carries elapsed_ms equal to the budget\r\n * (the countdown reports zero, so the spend is the whole budget). Restoring it\r\n * as an ordinary resume opens the section on 00:00 with no buzzer, because the\r\n * regulation mark it also carries tells the buzzer effect it has already\r\n * asked. The runner uses this to open such a row on the buzzer instead: the\r\n * answers are kept, the clock is not handed back, and the student is asked to\r\n * finish and score the section as it was sat.\r\n *\r\n * Untimed runs have no budget and are never "spent".\r\n */\r\nexport function isSpentDrillSession(\r\n  stored: DrillResumeSession | null,\r\n  budgetMs: number | undefined,\r\n): boolean {\r\n  if (!stored || !budgetMs || !Number.isFinite(budgetMs)) return false;\r\n  return (stored.elapsed_ms ?? 0) >= budgetMs;\r\n}\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 7.7 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '/**\r\n * Practice-set resume store for the Intro to LR practice drills.\r\n *\r\n * A student who starts a practice set and leaves — closes the tab, clicks away\r\n * to a lesson, signs out, comes back tomorrow on another machine — must land\r\n * back on the question they stopped on, with every answer they already gave\r\n * still marked. React state and URL params cannot do that: both die with the\r\n * page. So each practice set keeps one durable row per user in the shared\r\n * bootcamp_kv persistence (the same layer module_progress uses), written on\r\n * every answer and on every question move.\r\n *\r\n * These are standalone functions rather than a hook because the drill runner\r\n * (src/pages/Drill.tsx) lives outside the bootcamp\'s React tree — the same\r\n * reason recordPracticeCompletion.ts exists.\r\n *\r\n * Failure policy: reads degrade to null (a missing resume row must never block\r\n * starting a drill). Writes throw so the caller can surface a toast rather than\r\n * silently losing a student\'s place.\r\n */\r\n\r\nimport { getPersistence } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/persistence/factory\';\r\nimport { TABLES, PracticeSession, type PracticeSessionAttempt } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/persistence/records\';\r\nimport { now } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/lib/ids\';\r\nimport {\r\n  MAIN_CONCLUSION_PRACTICE_POOLS,\r\n  type PracticePool,\r\n} from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/data/practiceDrillPools\';\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Deterministic per-user, per-pool record id, so every read and write lands on\r\n * the same row without a lookup. Mirrors progressIdFor\'s FNV-1a approach but\r\n * hashes user + slug together.\r\n */\r\nexport function practiceSessionIdFor(userId: string, poolSlug: string): string {\r\n  const input = `${userId}::${poolSlug}`;\r\n  let h = 0x811c9dc5;\r\n  for (let i = 0; i < input.length; i++) {\r\n    h ^= input.charCodeAt(i);\r\n    h = Math.imul(h, 0x01000193);\r\n  }\r\n  let h2 = 0x811c9dc5;\r\n  for (let i = input.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\r\n    h2 ^= input.charCodeAt(i);\r\n    h2 = Math.imul(h2, 0x01000193);\r\n  }\r\n  const hex = (h >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, \'0\');\r\n  const hex2 = (h2 >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, \'0\');\r\n  return `00000000-0000-4000-8000-${hex}${hex2.slice(0, 4)}`;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * One writer per row at a time.\r\n *\r\n * Both writers below read the row and then write it, with an await in between.\r\n * The drill runner fires a snapshot on every answer and a completion at the\r\n * end, and nothing stopped a snapshot that had already read the row from\r\n * writing it AFTER the completion had — which put a finished section back to\r\n * in_progress. Chaining each row\'s writes behind the previous one keeps them\r\n * in the order they were asked for. Writes to different rows still overlap.\r\n */\r\nconst rowChains = new Map<string, Promise<unknown>>();\r\n\r\nasync function withRow<T>(id: string, fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {\r\n  const previous = rowChains.get(id) ?? Promise.resolve();\r\n  const run = previous.then(fn, fn);\r\n  rowChains.set(id, run.catch(() => undefined));\r\n  try {\r\n    return await run;\r\n  } finally {\r\n    if (rowChains.get(id) === run) rowChains.delete(id);\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface PracticeSessionSnapshot {\r\n  questionQueue: string[];\r\n  currentIndex: number;\r\n  attempts: PracticeSessionAttempt[];\r\n  /** Section clock already spent, for the surfaces that run one. */\r\n  elapsedMs?: number;\r\n  /** Answers that were already down when the clock ran out, if it did. */\r\n  regulationAnsweredQids?: string[];\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Read the stored session for one pool. Returns null when there is nothing to\r\n * resume, when the row is corrupt, or when persistence is unavailable — all of\r\n * which mean "start fresh", never "block the student".\r\n */\r\nexport async function loadPracticeSession(\r\n  userId: string,\r\n  poolSlug: string,\r\n): Promise<PracticeSession | null> {\r\n  if (!userId || !poolSlug) return null;\r\n  try {\r\n    const persist = getPersistence(userId);\r\n    // Wait for cloud hydration first, otherwise a cold load on a second device\r\n    // reports "nothing to resume" while the row is still in flight.\r\n    await persist.ready();\r\n    const raw = await persist.get<unknown>(TABLES.practice_sessions, practiceSessionIdFor(userId, poolSlug));\r\n    if (!raw) return null;\r\n    const parsed = PracticeSession.safeParse(raw);\r\n    if (!parsed.success) {\r\n      console.warn(\'[practice-session] stored row failed validation, ignoring\', {\r\n        poolSlug,\r\n        issues: parsed.error.issues,\r\n      });\r\n      return null;\r\n    }\r\n    return parsed.data;\r\n  } catch (e) {\r\n    console.warn(\'[practice-session] load failed, starting fresh\', { poolSlug, error: e });\r\n    return null;\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** Read the stored session for every practice pool, for the index UI. */\r\nexport async function loadAllPracticeSessions(\r\n  userId: string,\r\n): Promise<Record<string, PracticeSession>> {\r\n  const slugs = Object.keys(MAIN_CONCLUSION_PRACTICE_POOLS);\r\n  const rows = await Promise.all(slugs.map((slug) => loadPracticeSession(userId, slug)));\r\n  const out: Record<string, PracticeSession> = {};\r\n  rows.forEach((row, i) => {\r\n    if (row) out[slugs[i]] = row;\r\n  });\r\n  return out;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Write the student\'s current place in a practice set. Called after every\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 5.3 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "// exactly where they were: the frozen question queue, where they stopped, and\r\n// every answer already given. Survives tab close, sign-out, and device change\r\n// because it rides the same bootcamp_kv sync as module_progress.\r\nexport const PracticeSessionAttempt = z.object({\r\n  qid: z.string(),\r\n  selected_answer: z.string(),\r\n  correct: z.boolean(),\r\n  time_ms: z.number().int().min(0).default(0),\r\n  /**\r\n   * Everything a resumed run must not lose. A student who leaves a section\r\n   * half-sat and comes back should find the same record they left, not a\r\n   * flattened one: the answers they changed their mind about, how many times,\r\n   * and what they marked for review. All optional — rows written before this\r\n   * existed are still valid, they simply carry less.\r\n   */\r\n  switch_count: z.number().int().min(0).optional(),\r\n  flagged: z.boolean().optional(),\r\n  answer_trail: z\r\n    .array(z.object({ answer: z.string(), at_ms: z.number().min(0) }))\r\n    .optional(),\r\n  /**\r\n   * The answer was on screen (Answer or Explanation pressed) before or after\r\n   * this pick. Without it a resumed run could not tell a pick made after\r\n   * seeing the answer from a clean one. Optional: older rows read as false.\r\n   */\r\n  answer_revealed: z.boolean().optional(),\r\n});\r\nexport type PracticeSessionAttempt = z.infer<typeof PracticeSessionAttempt>;\r\n\r\nexport const PracticeSession = z.object({\r\n  id: UuidV4Schema,\r\n  user_id: UuidV4Schema,\r\n  pool_slug: z.string(),\r\n  completion_id: z.string(),\r\n  question_queue: z.array(z.string()),\r\n  current_index: z.number().int().min(0).default(0),\r\n  attempts: z.array(PracticeSessionAttempt).default([]),\r\n  status: z.enum(['in_progress', 'completed']).default('in_progress'),\r\n  /**\r\n   * How much of the section clock has already been spent. A timed section that\r\n   * resumed with a fresh 35:00 was handing back time the student had already\r\n   * used, which makes the score meaningless. Absent for anything untimed.\r\n   */\r\n  elapsed_ms: z.number().min(0).optional(),\r\n  /**\r\n   * Which questions already carried an answer when the section clock ran out.\r\n   * Kept so a section resumed during overtime still knows which side of the\r\n   * buzzer each answer belongs to; recomputing it on resume would quietly\r\n   * move every overtime answer back inside the clock.\r\n   */\r\n  regulation_answered_qids: z.array(z.string()).optional(),\r\n  started_at: IsoDateSchema,\r\n  updated_at: IsoDateSchema,\r\n});\r\nexport type PracticeSession = z.infer<typeof PracticeSession>;\r\n\r\nexport const TABLES = {\r\n  users: 'users',\r\n  module_progress: 'module_progress',\r\n  lesson_progress: 'lesson_progress',\r\n * answer and every question move, so it must stay cheap — the persistence layer\r\n * writes to the local cache synchronously and debounces the Supabase upsert.\r\n *\r\n * The row is always written as in_progress: this is the live run, and a\r\n * completed row under the same key means a previous run of the same pool that\r\n * this one is replacing. A caller that must not write over a finished run —\r\n * a homework assignment, whose row is that one sitting — stops calling before\r\n * it gets here (see genericFinalizedRef in Drill.tsx).\r\n */\r\nexport async function savePracticeSessionSnapshot(\r\n  userId: string,\r\n  pool: Pick<PracticePool, 'slug' | 'completionId'>,\r\n  snapshot: PracticeSessionSnapshot,\r\n): Promise<void> {\r\n  if (!userId || !pool?.slug) return;\r\n  if (snapshot.questionQueue.length === 0) return;\r\n\r\n  const id = practiceSessionIdFor(userId, pool.slug);\r\n  await withRow(id, async () => {\r\n    const persist = getPersistence(userId);\r\n    const existing = await loadPracticeSession(userId, pool.slug);\r\n\r\n    const record = PracticeSession.parse({\r\n      id,\r\n      user_id: userId,\r\n      pool_slug: pool.slug,\r\n      completion_id: pool.completionId,\r\n      question_queue: snapshot.questionQueue,\r\n      current_index: Math.min(\r\n        Math.max(snapshot.currentIndex, 0),\r\n        Math.max(snapshot.questionQueue.length - 1, 0),\r\n      ),\r\n      attempts: snapshot.attempts,\r\n      status: 'in_progress',\r\n      // Never hand back time already spent: a snapshot with no clock keeps\r\n      // whatever the row already knew rather than clearing it.\r\n      elapsed_ms: snapshot.elapsedMs ?? existing?.elapsed_ms,\r\n      regulation_answered_qids:\r\n        snapshot.regulationAnsweredQids ?? existing?.regulation_answered_qids,\r\n      started_at: existing?.started_at ?? now(),\r\n      updated_at: now(),\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    await persist.set(TABLES.practice_sessions, id, record);\r\n  });\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Mark the set finished. The row is kept rather than deleted so the index can\r\n * show what was answered and how it went after completion.\r\n */\r\nexport async function completePracticeSession(\r\n  userId: string,\r\n  pool: Pick<PracticePool, 'slug' | 'completionId'>,\r\n  snapshot: PracticeSessionSnapshot,\r\n): Promise<void> {\r\n  if (!userId || !pool?.slug) return;\r\n\r\n  const id = practiceSessionIdFor(userId, pool.slug);\r\n  await withRow(id, async () => {\r\n    const persist = getPersistence(userId);\r\n    const existing = await loadPracticeSession(userId, pool.slug);\r\n\r\n    const record = PracticeSession.parse({\r\n      id,\r\n      user_id: userId,\r\n      pool_slug: pool.slug,\r\n      completion_id: pool.completionId,\r\n      question_queue: snapshot.questionQueue.length\r\n        ? snapshot.questionQueue\r\n        : existing?.question_queue ?? [],\r\n      current_index: Math.max(snapshot.questionQueue.length - 1, 0),\r\n      attempts: snapshot.attempts.length ? snapshot.attempts : existing?.attempts ?? [],\r\n      status: 'completed',\r\n      elapsed_ms: snapshot.elapsedMs ?? existing?.elapsed_ms,\r\n      regulation_answered_qids:\r\n        snapshot.regulationAnsweredQids ?? existing?.regulation_answered_qids,\r\n      started_at: existing?.started_at ?? now(),\r\n      updated_at: now(),\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    await persist.set(TABLES.practice_sessions, id, record);\r\n  });\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** Clear the row so a re-run of a finished set starts from question 1. */\r\nexport async function resetPracticeSession(userId: string, poolSlug: string): Promise<void> {\r\n  if (!userId || !poolSlug) return;\r\n  const persist = getPersistence(userId);\r\n  await persist.remove(TABLES.practice_sessions, practiceSessionIdFor(userId, poolSlug));\r\n}\r\n"}]

});\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Deterministic per-user, per-pool record id, so every read and write lands on\\r\\n * the same row without a lookup. Mirrors progressIdFor\'s FNV-1a approach but\\r\\n * hashes user + slug together.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function practiceSessionIdFor(userId: string, poolSlug: string): string {\\r\\n  const input = `${userId}::${poolSlug}`;\\r\\n  let h = 0x811c9dc5;\\r\\n  for (let i = 0; i < input.length; i++) {\\r\\n    h ^= input.charCodeAt(i);\\r\\n    h = Math.imul(h, 0x01000193);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  let h2 = 0x811c9dc5;\\r\\n  for (let i = input.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\\r\\n    h2 ^= input.charCodeAt(i);\\r\\n    h2 = Math.imul(h2, 0x01000193);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  const hex = (h >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, \'0\');\\r\\n  const hex2 = (h2 >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, \'0\');\\r\\n  return `00000000-0000-4000-8000-${hex}${hex2.slice(0, 4)}`;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * One writer per row at a time.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Both writers below read the row and then write it, with an await in between.\\r\\n * The drill runner fires a snapshot on every answer and a completion at the\\r\\n * end, and nothing stopped a snapshot that had already read the row from\\r\\n * writing it AFTER the completion had — which put a finished section back to\\r\\n * in_progress. Chaining each row\'s writes behind the previous one keeps them\\r\\n * in the order they were asked for. Writes to different rows still overlap.\\r\\n */\\r\\nconst rowChains = new Map<string, Promise<unknown>>();\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function withRow<T>(id: string, fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {\\r\\n  const previous = rowChains.get(id) ?? Promise.resolve();\\r\\n  const run = previous.then(fn, fn);\\r\\n  rowChains.set(id, run.catch(() => undefined));\\r\\n  try {\\r\\n    return await run;\\r\\n  } finally {\\r\\n    if (rowChains.get(id) === run) rowChains.delete(id);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface PracticeSessionSnapshot {\\r\\n  questionQueue: string[];\\r\\n  currentIndex: number;\\r\\n  attempts: PracticeSessionAttempt[];\\r\\n  /** Section clock already spent, for the surfaces that run one. */\\r\\n  elapsedMs?: number;\\r\\n  /** Answers that were already down when the clock ran out, if it did. */\\r\\n  regulationAnsweredQids?: string[];\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Read the stored session for one pool. Returns null when there is nothing to\\r\\n * resume, when the row is corrupt, or when persistence is unavailable — all of\\r\\n * which mean \\"start fresh\\", never \\"block the student\\".\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport async function loadPracticeSession(\\r\\n  userId: string,\\r\\n  poolSlug: string,\\r\\n): Promise<PracticeSession | null> {\\r\\n  if (!userId || !poolSlug) return null;\\r\\n  try {\\r\\n    const persist = getPersistence(userId);\\r\\n    // Wait for cloud hydration first, otherwise a cold load on a second device\\r\\n    // reports \\"nothing to resume\\" while the row is still in flight.\\r\\n    await persist.ready();\\r\\n    const raw = await persist.get<unknown>(TABLES.practice_sessions, practiceSessionIdFor(userId, poolSlug));\\r\\n    if (!raw) return null;\\r\\n    const parsed = PracticeSession.safeParse(raw);\\r\\n    if (!parsed.success) {\\r\\n      console.warn(\'[practice-session] stored row failed validation, ignoring\', {\\r\\n        poolSlug,\\r\\n        issues: parsed.error.issues,\\r\\n      });\\r\\n      return null;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    const migrated = migratePracticeSession(parsed.data);\\r\\n    if (migrated !== parsed.data) {\\r\\n      try {\\r\\n        await persist.set(TABLES.practice_sessions, migrated.id, migrated);\\r\\n      } catch (error) {\\r\\n        // A sync failure must not hide an otherwise usable local resume row.\\r\\n        console.warn(\'[practice-session] migration write failed\', { poolSlug, error });\\r\\n      }\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    return migrated;\\r\\n  } catch (e) {\\r\\n    console.warn(\'[practice-session] load failed, starting fresh\', { poolSlug, error: e });\\r\\n    return null;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Read the stored session for every practice pool, for the index UI. */\\r\\n"}}}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': [{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 2.7 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "/**\r\n * Practice-set resume store for the Intro to LR practice drills.\r\n *\r\n * A student who starts a practice set and leaves — closes the tab, clicks away\r\n * to a lesson, signs out, comes back tomorrow on another machine — must land\r\n * back on the question they stopped on, with every answer they already gave\r\n * still marked. React state and URL params cannot do that: both die with the\r\n * page. So each practice set keeps one durable row per user in the shared\r\n * bootcamp_kv persistence (the same layer module_progress uses), written on\r\n * every answer and on every question move.\r\n *\r\n * These are standalone functions rather than a hook because the drill runner\r\n * (src/pages/Drill.tsx) lives outside the bootcamp's React tree — the same\r\n * reason recordPracticeCompletion.ts exists.\r\n *\r\n * Failure policy: reads degrade to null (a missing resume row must never block\r\n * starting a drill). Writes throw so the caller can surface a toast rather than\r\n * silently losing a student's place.\r\n */\r\n\r\nimport { getPersistence } from '@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/persistence/factory';\r\nimport { TABLES, PracticeSession, type PracticeSessionAttempt } from '@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/persistence/records';\r\nimport { now } from '@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/lib/ids';\r\nimport {\r\n  MAIN_CONCLUSION_PRACTICE_POOLS,\r\n  type PracticePool,\r\n} from '@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/data/practiceDrillPools';\r\n\r\nexport const PRACTICE_SESSION_DATA_VERSION = 2;\r\n\r\nconst PT118_S1_ANSWERS: Record<number, string> = {\r\n  1: 'A', 2: 'C', 3: 'B', 4: 'C', 6: 'D', 7: 'B', 8: 'A',\r\n  9: 'E', 10: 'A', 11: 'C', 12: 'C', 13: 'E', 14: 'E',\r\n  15: 'E', 16: 'C', 17: 'B', 18: 'D', 19: 'A', 20: 'D',\r\n  21: 'A', 22: 'E', 23: 'C', 24: 'B', 25: 'C', 26: 'E',\r\n};\r\n\r\nfunction migratePt118Qid(qid: string): string | null {\r\n  const match = /^PT118-S([1234])-Q(\\d+)$/.exec(qid);\r\n  if (!match) return qid;\r\n  const section = Number(match[1]);\r\n  const number = Number(match[2]);\r\n  if (section === 3 && number === 22) return null;\r\n  if (section === 1 && number >= 5 && number <= 25) return `PT118-S1-Q${number + 1}`;\r\n  return qid;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction pt118CorrectAnswer(qid: string): string | undefined {\r\n  const match = /^PT118-S([12])-Q(\\d+)$/.exec(qid);\r\n  if (!match) return undefined;\r\n  const section = Number(match[1]);\r\n  const number = Number(match[2]);\r\n  if (section === 1) return PT118_S1_ANSWERS[number];\r\n  if (section === 2) return ({ 1: 'D', 5: 'A' } as Record<number, string>)[number];\r\n  return undefined;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** Remap pre-repair PT118 resume IDs once, preserving the student's place by content. */\r\nexport function migratePracticeSession(row: PracticeSession): PracticeSession {\r\n  if ((row.data_version ?? 0) >= PRACTICE_SESSION_DATA_VERSION) return row;\r\n  const oldCurrent = row.question_queue[row.current_index];\r\n  const mappedCurrent = oldCurrent ? migratePt118Qid(oldCurrent) : null;\r\n  const seen = new Set<string>();\r\n  const questionQueue = row.question_queue.flatMap((qid) => {\r\n    const mapped = migratePt118Qid(qid);\r\n    if (!mapped || seen.has(mapped)) return [];\r\n    seen.add(mapped);\r\n    return [mapped];\r\n  });\r\n  const seenAttempts = new Set<string>();\r\n  const attempts = row.attempts.flatMap((attempt) => {\r\n    const qid = migratePt118Qid(attempt.qid);\r\n    if (!qid || seenAttempts.has(qid)) return [];\r\n    seenAttempts.add(qid);\r\n    const answer = pt118CorrectAnswer(qid);\r\n    return [{ ...attempt, qid, correct: answer ? attempt.selected_answer === answer : attempt.correct }];\r\n  });\r\n  const currentIndex = mappedCurrent && questionQueue.includes(mappedCurrent)\r\n    ? questionQueue.indexOf(mappedCurrent)\r\n    : Math.min(row.current_index, Math.max(questionQueue.length - 1, 0));\r\n  const regulationAnsweredQids = row.regulation_answered_qids && [\r\n    ...new Set(row.regulation_answered_qids.map(migratePt118Qid).filter((qid): qid is string => !!qid)),\r\n  ];\r\n  return PracticeSession.parse({\r\n    ...row,\r\n    data_version: PRACTICE_SESSION_DATA_VERSION,\r\n    question_queue: questionQueue,\r\n    current_index: currentIndex,\r\n    attempts,\r\n    regulation_answered_qids: regulationAnsweredQids,\r\n  });\r\n}\r\n"}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 6.6 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "\r\n/**\r\n * What a run's sections actually run for. Reads the seconds resolved when the\r\n * run was created rather than recomputing, so changing the timing setting\r\n * halfway through a two-hour sitting cannot move the clock under it. Runs\r\n * created before this was stored fall back to the old absolute reading.\r\n */\r\nexport function practiceTestRunSectionSeconds(run: PracticeTestRun): number | null {\r\n  return run.sectionSeconds !== undefined\r\n    ? run.sectionSeconds\r\n    : practiceTestSectionSeconds(run.pace);\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport type PracticeTestLegKind = 'lr' | 'rc';\r\n\r\nexport interface PracticeTestLeg {\r\n  kind: PracticeTestLegKind;\r\n  /** The section number it carried on the real PrepTest. */\r\n  section: number;\r\n  /** Questions in this leg — what it contributes to the raw score. */\r\n  questionCount: number;\r\n  /** RC only: the four passages, in published order. */\r\n  passageIds?: number[];\r\n  /** ISO stamp set when the student begins this leg. */\r\n  startedAt?: string;\r\n  /** ISO stamp set when its result is read back. */\r\n  completedAt?: string;\r\n  correct?: number;\r\n  answered?: number;\r\n  timeMs?: number;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport type PracticeTestStatus = 'in-progress' | 'complete' | 'abandoned';\r\n\r\nexport interface PracticeTestRun {\r\n  id: string;\r\n  pt: number;\r\n  pace: PracticeTestPaceId;\r\n  /**\r\n   * Seconds per section for this sitting, resolved against the student's own\r\n   * standard clock when the run was created. Optional because runs stored\r\n   * before it existed do not carry it; read it through\r\n   * `practiceTestRunSectionSeconds`, never directly.\r\n   */\r\n  sectionSeconds?: number | null;\r\n  legs: PracticeTestLeg[];\r\n  /** Index of the leg the student is on. Equals legs.length when finished. */\r\n  currentLeg: number;\r\n  /** Legs completed before the break. The real test breaks after two. */\r\n  breakAfterLeg: number;\r\n  /** True once the student has taken (or skipped) the break. */\r\n  breakTaken: boolean;\r\n  /** ISO stamp set when the break clock started, so it survives a reload. */\r\n  breakStartedAt?: string;\r\n  status: PracticeTestStatus;\r\n  createdAt: string;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface PracticeTestBlueprint {\r\n  pt: number;\r\n  legs: Omit<PracticeTestLeg, 'startedAt' | 'completedAt' | 'correct' | 'answered' | 'timeMs'>[];\r\n  questionCount: number;\r\n  /**\r\n   * False when the Reading Comprehension leg carries fewer questions than a\r\n   * real section does. The sitting is still worth doing — the reading load is\r\n   * identical and reading is what the clock is really measuring — but the raw\r\n   * total is smaller, and every surface that offers this has to say so.\r\n   */\r\n  rcFullLength: boolean;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Assemble every PrepTest that can be sat end to end.\r\n *\r\n * A test needs two full LR sections and one complete four-passage RC section\r\n * from the same PrepTest. It does NOT require the RC section to carry a real\r\n * section's worth of questions, and that is a deliberate reversal: gating on\r\n * question count left 11 of 58 PrepTests sittable, because the bank publishes\r\n * only the RC questions that carry a written explanation.\r\n *\r\n\r\nfunction storeKey(id: string) {\r\n  return `${STORE_PREFIX}${id}`;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction newRunId(): string {\r\n  const c = globalThis.crypto;\r\n  if (c && 'randomUUID' in c) return c.randomUUID();\r\n  return `pt-${Date.now()}-${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1e6)}`;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function createPracticeTestRun(\r\n  blueprint: PracticeTestBlueprint,\r\n  pace: PracticeTestPaceId,\r\n  /**\r\n   * Seconds per section, already resolved against the student's own clock.\r\n   * Stored on the run so the sitting keeps the clock it started with even if\r\n   * the timing setting changes while it is in progress.\r\n   */\r\n  sectionSeconds: number | null = practiceTestSectionSeconds(pace),\r\n): PracticeTestRun {\r\n  const run: PracticeTestRun = {\r\n    id: newRunId(),\r\n    pt: blueprint.pt,\r\n    pace,\r\n    sectionSeconds,\r\n    legs: blueprint.legs.map((l) => ({ ...l })),\r\n    currentLeg: 0,\r\n    // Two sections, then the break — the shape of the real sitting. A test that\r\n    // somehow has fewer legs breaks after the second-to-last rather than never.\r\n    breakAfterLeg: Math.min(2, Math.max(1, blueprint.legs.length - 1)),\r\n    breakTaken: false,\r\n    status: 'in-progress',\r\n    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),\r\n  };\r\n  savePracticeTestRun(run);\r\n  return run;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function savePracticeTestRun(run: PracticeTestRun): void {\r\n  try {\r\n    localStorage.setItem(storeKey(run.id), JSON.stringify(run));\r\n  } catch {\r\n    // A full or disabled store must not take the sitting down. The student\r\n    // keeps answering; only resume-after-reload is lost.\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function loadPracticeTestRun(id: string): PracticeTestRun | null {\r\n  try {\r\n    const raw = localStorage.getItem(storeKey(id));\r\n    if (!raw) return null;\r\n    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as PracticeTestRun;\r\n    if (!parsed || !Array.isArray(parsed.legs)) return null;\r\n    return parsed;\r\n  } catch {\r\n    return null;\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function listPracticeTestRuns(): PracticeTestRun[] {\r\n  const runs: PracticeTestRun[] = [];\r\n  try {\r\n    for (let i = 0; i < localStorage.length; i += 1) {\r\n      const key = localStorage.key(i);\r\n      if (!key || !key.startsWith(STORE_PREFIX)) continue;\r\n      const run = loadPracticeTestRun(key.slice(STORE_PREFIX.length));\r\n      if (run) runs.push(run);\r\n    }\r\n  } catch {\r\n    return [];\r\n  }\r\n  return runs.sort((a, b) => b.createdAt.localeCompare(a.createdAt));\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function deletePracticeTestRun(id: string): void {\r\n  try {\r\n    localStorage.removeItem(storeKey(id));\r\n  } catch {\r\n    /* nothing to clean up */\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\r\n/* Derived views                                                               */\r\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\r\n\r\nexport function practiceTestRaw(run: PracticeTestRun): { correct: number; total: number } {\r\n  return run.legs.reduce(\r\n    (acc, leg) => ({\r\n      correct: acc.correct + (leg.correct ?? 0),\r\n      total: acc.total + leg.questionCount,\r\n    }),\r\n    { correct: 0, total: 0 },\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function practiceTestScaled(run: PracticeTestRun): number {\r\n  const { correct, total } = practiceTestRaw(run);\r\n  return estimateScaledScore(correct, total);\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function legLabel(leg: PracticeTestLeg): string {\r\n  return leg.kind === 'lr' ? 'Logical Reasoning' : 'Reading Comprehension';\r\n}\r\n"}]

## assistant

I found a separate scoring error while tracing the sitting builder: it selects the lowest numbered two LR sections. For PT118, that means it currently includes **Section 3, which the PDF marks unscored**, and leaves out scored Section 4. I’m correcting the section selection as part of this repair; otherwise the answer fixes would still produce an invalid PT118 score.

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 6.0 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '\r\n      <div className="testing-actions">\r\n        <Button onClick={onExit}>Back to the Testing Centre</Button>\r\n      </div>\r\n    </div>\r\n  );\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport default function PracticeTestRunPage() {\r\n  const { runId } = useParams<{ runId: string }>();\r\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\r\n  const { user } = useAuth();\r\n  const [run, setRun] = React.useState<Run | null | undefined>(undefined);\r\n  const [checking, setChecking] = React.useState(false);\r\n  const [noResult, setNoResult] = React.useState(false);\r\n\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    setRun(runId ? loadPracticeTestRun(runId) : null);\r\n  }, [runId]);\r\n\r\n  const commit = React.useCallback((next: Run) => {\r\n    savePracticeTestRun(next);\r\n    setRun(next);\r\n  }, []);\r\n\r\n  /**\r\n   * Coming back from a leg. Runs once per return: the leg that was started has\r\n   * either produced a row or it has not, and there is nothing to poll for.\r\n   */\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (!run || !user) return;\r\n    const leg = run.legs[run.currentLeg];\r\n    if (!leg || !leg.startedAt || leg.completedAt) return;\r\n    let cancelled = false;\r\n\r\n    setChecking(true);\r\n    void readLegResult(user.id, run, leg)\r\n      .then((result) => {\r\n        if (cancelled) return;\r\n        if (!result) {\r\n          setNoResult(true);\r\n          return;\r\n        }\r\n        const legs = run.legs.map((l, i) =>\r\n          i === run.currentLeg\r\n            ? {\r\n                ...l,\r\n                completedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\r\n                correct: result.correct,\r\n                answered: result.answered,\r\n                timeMs: result.timeMs,\r\n              }\r\n            : l,\r\n        );\r\n        const nextLeg = run.currentLeg + 1;\r\n        commit({\r\n          ...run,\r\n          legs,\r\n          currentLeg: nextLeg,\r\n          status: nextLeg >= legs.length ? "complete" : "in-progress",\r\n        });\r\n      })\r\n      .finally(() => {\r\n        if (!cancelled) setChecking(false);\r\n      });\r\n\r\n    return () => {\r\n      cancelled = true;\r\n    };\r\n  }, [run, user, commit]);\r\n\r\n  /** The break clock starts the moment the break becomes due, not on render. */\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (!run || run.status !== "in-progress") return;\r\n    if (run.breakTaken || run.currentLeg !== run.breakAfterLeg) return;\r\n    if (run.breakStartedAt) return;\r\n    commit({ ...run, breakStartedAt: new Date().toISOString() });\r\n  }, [run, commit]);\r\n\r\n  const begin = () => {\r\n    if (!run) return;\r\n    const leg = run.legs[run.currentLeg];\r\n    if (!leg) return;\r\n    const startedAt = new Date().toISOString();\r\n    const next: Run = {\r\n      ...run,\r\n      legs: run.legs.map((l, i) => (i === run.currentLeg ? { ...l, startedAt } : l)),\r\n    };\r\n    // Stamped and saved BEFORE navigating: the readback matches on this\r\n    // timestamp, and a leg that starts without one can never be scored.\r\n    savePracticeTestRun(next);\r\n    setRun(next);\r\n    setNoResult(false);\r\n\r\n    if (leg.kind === "lr") {\r\n      const launch = lrLegLaunch(next, leg, next.currentLeg);\r\n      navigate(launch.pathname, { state: launch.state });\r\n    } else {\r\n      navigate(rcLegHref(next, leg, next.currentLeg));\r\n    }\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  if (run === undefined) {\r\n    return (\r\n      <div\r\n        className="flex items-center gap-3 py-10 text-sm"\r\n        role="status"\r\n        aria-live="polite"\r\n      >\r\n        <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" aria-hidden />\r\n        Loading your practice test…\r\n      </div>\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  if (!run) {\r\n    return (\r\n      <>\r\n        <h1 className="classroom-title">Practice test not found</h1>\r\n        <p className="testing-lede">\r\n          This sitting is not on this device. Practice tests in progress are kept\r\n          in the browser you started them in.\r\n        </p>\r\n        <Button onClick={() => navigate("/testing")}>Back to the Testing Centre</Button>\r\n      </>\r\n    );\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  const clock = formatPracticeTestClock(practiceTestRunSectionSeconds(run));\r\n  const paceLabel = practiceTestPaceLabel(run.pace);\r\n  const leg = run.legs[run.currentLeg];\r\n  const breakDue =\r\n    !run.breakTaken && run.currentLeg === run.breakAfterLeg && run.status === "in-progress";\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <>\r\n      <h1 className="classroom-title">Practice Test {run.pt}</h1>\r\n      <p className="testing-lede">\r\n        {run.legs.length} scored sections · {paceLabel}\r\n        {clock ? ` · ${clock} a section` : ""}\r\n      </p>\r\n\r\n      {run.status !== "complete" && (\r\n      <div className="testing-shape">\r\n        {run.legs.map((l, i) => (\r\n          <div\r\n            className="testing-shape-row"\r\n            data-state={\r\n              l.completedAt ? "done" : i === run.currentLeg ? "current" : undefined\r\n            }\r\n            key={`${l.kind}-${l.section}`}\r\n          >\r\n            <b>Section {i + 1}</b>\r\n            <span>{legLabel(l)}</span>\r\n            <em>\r\n              {/* No number until the whole sitting is over. Knowing section 1\'s\r\n                  score changes how the rest is sat, which is the difference\r\n                  between a practice test and three drills. */}\r\n              {l.completedAt ? \'Finished\' : `${l.questionCount} questions`}\r\n            </em>\r\n          </div>\r\n        ))}\r\n      </div>\r\n      )}\r\n\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 1.9 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '/**\r\n * One practice test, in progress.\r\n *\r\n * This page is the invigilator, not the exam. It says which section is next,\r\n * starts it, runs the break, reads each leg\'s result back out of the tables the\r\n * runners write, and scores the sitting at the end. Every actual question is\r\n * answered somewhere else, in the same runner the student uses outside a test.\r\n *\r\n * The result readback is deliberately conservative: a leg with no matching row\r\n * is reported as unfinished rather than scored as zero. A zero here would read\r\n * on the score report as "answered everything wrong", which is a different and\r\n * much more discouraging claim than "you walked away from section 2".\r\n */\r\nimport * as React from "react";\r\nimport { useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";\r\nimport { Loader2 } from "lucide-react";\r\nimport { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";\r\nimport { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";\r\nimport {\r\n  formatPracticeTestClock,\r\n  legLabel,\r\n  loadPracticeTestRun,\r\n  practiceTestPaceLabel,\r\n  practiceTestRunSectionSeconds,\r\n  practiceTestRaw,\r\n  practiceTestScaled,\r\n  savePracticeTestRun,\r\n  PRACTICE_TEST_BREAK_SECONDS,\r\n  type PracticeTestRun as Run,\r\n} from "@/lib/practiceTest";\r\nimport { lrLegLaunch, rcLegHref, readLegResult } from "@/lib/practiceTestRunner";\r\nimport "./testing-centre.css";\r\n\r\nfunction mmss(seconds: number) {\r\n  const s = Math.max(0, Math.floor(seconds));\r\n  return `${Math.floor(s / 60)}:${String(s % 60).padStart(2, "0")}`;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * What a ticking clock says out loud.\r\n *\r\n * The digits change twice a second, and a live region wired straight to them\r\n * would talk over itself for ten minutes without pause. Coarse steps instead:\r\n * whole minutes while minutes remain, then ten-second steps inside the last\r\n * one. The string changes only when the step does, so the region speaks only\r\n * when there is something new to say.\r\n */\r\nfunction breakAnnouncement(remaining: number): string {\r\n  const seconds = Math.ceil(remaining);\r\n  if (seconds <= 0) return "The break is over. Continue when you are ready.";\r\n  if (seconds <= 60) return `${Math.ceil(seconds / 10) * 10} seconds of the break left.`;\r\n  const minutes = Math.ceil(seconds / 60);\r\n  return `${minutes} ${minutes === 1 ? "minute" : "minutes"} of the break left.`;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** The break, on its own clock. Skippable — it is a break, not a gate. */\r\nfunction BreakScreen({ run, onDone }: { run: Run; onDone: () => void }) {\r\n  const startedAt = run.breakStartedAt ?
